CMPXCHG8B (M64) - Latency

Operands

  • Operand 1 (r/w): Memory
  • Operand 2 (r/w, suppressed): Register (EDX)
  • Operand 3 (r/w, suppressed): Register (EAX)
  • Operand 4 (r, suppressed): Register (ECX)
  • Operand 5 (r, suppressed): Register (EBX)
  • Operand 6 (w, suppressed): Flags (ZF: w)

Latency operand 1 → 1 (address, base register): ≤45

Latency operand 1 → 1 (address, index register): ≤15

Latency operand 1 → 1 (memory): 9

Latency operand 1 → 2 (address, base register): 9

Latency operand 1 → 2 (address, index register): 8

Latency operand 1 → 2 (memory): ≤8

Latency operand 1 → 3 (address, base register): 9

Latency operand 1 → 3 (address, index register): 8

Latency operand 1 → 3 (memory): ≤8

Latency operand 1 → 6 (address, base register): 11

Latency operand 1 → 6 (address, index register): 10

Latency operand 1 → 6 (memory): ≤11

Latency operand 2 → 1: ≤9

Latency operand 2 → 2: 4

Latency operand 2 → 3: 5

Latency operand 2 → 6: 7

Latency operand 3 → 1: ≤8

Latency operand 3 → 2: 3

Latency operand 3 → 3: 4

Latency operand 3 → 6: 6

Latency operand 4 → 1: ≤8

Latency operand 4 → 2: 1

Latency operand 4 → 3: 0

Latency operand 4 → 6: 2

Latency operand 5 → 1: ≤7

Latency operand 5 → 2: 1

Latency operand 5 → 3: 2

Latency operand 5 → 6: 0

Latency operand 1 → 1 (address, base register): ≤45
